# Run Analysis: 2026-05-30T09-16Z

**Fetch time:** 2026-05-30T09:16:00.880375Z
**Total URLs in sitemap:** 1311
**Added:** 5 | **Updated:** 826 | **Removed:** 28
**Anomalies:** 0 | **Fetch failures:** 0

## Summary

**Headline story: Rosalind Biodefense.** OpenAI launched a dedicated biodefense AI initiative named Rosalind Biodefense (named after Rosalind Franklin), including GPT-Rosalind (a purpose-built life sciences model), a developer application program, and expanded government/allied partner access. The Life Sciences industry page was redesigned around this launch. Two new customer stories (Boston Children's Hospital for rare disease diagnosis; Braintrust for Codex-powered feature development) also appeared. A safety publication addressed how to design trustworthy third-party model evaluations.

**826 "updated" pages:** Nearly all reflect a site-wide CMS cascade where the "related content" carousels on hundreds of customer story and index pages were updated to feature the new Boston Children's Hospital and Braintrust stories. Actual content changes on those pages are minimal (typically just the carousel section).

**28 removed pages:** Most notably the entire DeployCo section (which was 404ing yesterday and is now fully gone), plus /agent-platform/, /science/, /foundation/, /safety/evaluations-hub/, /advertisers/, and legacy policy pages.
